<script>
$(document).ready(function(){
    var keypressed = false;
    $("#keyword_top").unbind("keypress").keypress(function(e){
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            keypressed = true;
        }
    });
    $("#keyword_top").unbind("keyup").keyup(function(e){
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && keypressed) {
            $('#search-btn-top').click();
        }
        keypressed = false;
    });
});
</script>
